Let's consider a nonlinear optimization with low-dimensional radial basis / spline using many random restarts. Since the space of smooth functions is infinite, but optimum may be piecewise linear/constant. We can discretize and use CMA-ES on a smaller reduced dimension: e.g., h as convex combination of basis functions (splines) plus active set. But previous full-dim local search may get stuck due many dimensions; global low-dim may find better basin.

Another angle: The objective is quasiconvex in the maximum? For fixed h, the maximum is over shifts. We can solve via iterative reweighting: minimize ∑ λ_k c_k where λ_k are inverse of slack, i.d. "convex-concave". But c_k bilinear. Could use alternating: fix h, compute active shifts; solve for h to reduce active shifts? For a set of active shifts, if c_k approximately linear? Not.

Could use "benders"/cutting plane? The objective is max of bilinear functions; we can bound by considering affine upper envelope? For each h, c_k. Since c_k is a quadratic form. If we linearize c_k at current h: c_k(h) ≈ c_k(h0)+∇c_k·(h-h0). Minimizing maximum of these linear majorants is an LP/SOCP (quadratically upper?). But c_k is indefinite (since cross product -h_i h_j), so first-order not globally upper. But for small steps, we can do SQP: solve a trust region subproblem with quadratic approximations. Implement? Maybe too much.

Could use scipy.optimize.minimize with method='trust-constr' and Hessian? Bilinear Hessian constant; we can supply gradient. trust-constr can handle nonconvex? It uses trust region reflective Bon Пригла? It may find local minima.

Let's craft exact evaluation function:
def compute_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(dx * np.max(corr))

Need ensure feasibility: sum h target. Could project.

Projection robust:
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
    t=n/2.0
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=t-h.sum()
    # iterative distribute to not-at-bound
    for _ in range(100):
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
       if diff>0: mask=h<1-1e-12 else mask=h>1e-12
       if not any: break
       add=diff/np.sum(mask)
       # to avoid overshoot, add = np.sign(diff)*min(abs(add), min slack? maybe simple)
       h[mask]+=add
       diff=t-h.sum()
    # final exact by adjusting one interior or average
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=t-h.sum()
    # if diff tiny, adjust all slightly
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       # distribute uniformly if possible (target in [0,n]) by definition
       h += diff/n
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       # one more: if clip changed, adjust interior
       for _ in range(10):
          diff=t-h.sum()
          if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
          if diff>0:
             mask=h<1-1e-15
          else:
             mask=h>1e-15
          if not np.any(mask):
             # adjust all?
             h += diff/n
          else:
             # add limited by max slack
             if diff>0:
                slack=(1-h[mask])
                cap=diff/np.sum(mask)
                # if cap > min slack, maybe distribute to those with max slack? simpler h[mask]+=diff/sum, clip
             h[mask]+=diff/np.sum(mask)
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h

This may not yield exact sum if all at bounds, but target n/2 feasible so there is at least half interior? If all 0/1 with exactly n/2 ones, sum exact. If after clip not exact due floating, we can do final: find indices not exactly 0/1, add diff/count. Should be fine for n/2.

For binary exact, use even n and int count.

Bitset binary search:
def bits_from_h(h, n):
    # threshold 0.5 adjust count
    h=np.asarray(h)
    # sort values to choose n/2 largest? For binary projection preserving sum: choose top n/2 indices by h.
    target=n//2
    idx=np.argpartition(h, target-1)[:target] if target>0 else []
    bits=0
    for i in idx: bits |= 1 << i
    return bits
But top n/2 threshold may include arbitrary tie. For n up to 512, loop. Use np.flatnonzero if h>0.5 adjust. Top by argpartition is good.

def binary_bits_to_array(bits, n):
    h=np.zeros(n)
    for i in range(n): h[i]= (bits>>i)&1
    return h

def eval_bits(bits, n):
    comp = ((1<<n)-1) ^ bits
    maxc=0
    # maybe k=0 zero
    for k in range(1,n):
        # A: h_i=1, h_{i+k}=0 => bits & (comp >> k). Note comp bits j, shifted down: bit i = comp_{i+k}. valid i 0..n-1-k; higher bits of comp>>k may include comp beyond? If comp has high bits maybe shifted into lower; but bits mask lower n bits, and for i<=n-k-1, i+k<n. bits has 0 above n-1, so & okay. For i>n-k-1, bits_i=0. Good.
        a = bits & (comp >> k)
        ca=a.bit_count()
        b = comp & (bits >> k)
        cb=b.bit_count()
        m=ca if ca>cb else cb
        if m>maxc: maxc=m
    c = 2.0 * maxc / n
    return c, maxc

For k=0, ca = bits & comp =0.

SLSQP optimization function:
def run_slsqp(h0,n,beta,deadline):
    set globals _G_N=n, _G_BETA=beta, _G_TARGET=n/2, _G_DEADLINE=deadline
    h0=project_feasible(h0,n)
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':slsqp_con}]
    try:
       res=minimize(slsp_obj,h0,jac=slsqp_jac,bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,method='SLSQP',options={'maxiter':400,'ftol':1e-12,'iprint':0},callback=slsqp_callback)
       h=project_feasible(res.x,n)
    except Exception:
       h=project_feasible(h0,n)
    return h

But SLSQP requires Jacobian shape (n,) okay. Constraint fun returns scalar. It may call callback after each iteration; raising RuntimeError inside will break. Need ensure not too many saves.

Custom Adam with soft weights:
def adam_polish(h0,n,deadline,lr=0.02,beta=30.0,maxiters=2000):
    h=project_feasible(h0,n).copy()
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h)
    m_state=np.zeros(n); v_state=np.zeros(n); t=0
    b1=0.9;b2=0.999; eps=1e-12
    for it in range(maxiters):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       dx=2.0/n
       corr=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
       cC=corr*dx
       C=cC.max()
       if C<best_c: best_c=C; best_h=h.copy()
       # weights softmax with beta; if beta too high, use top K
       m=cC.max()
       if beta>0:
          e=np.exp(beta*(cC-m)); s=e.sum(); w=e/s
       else:
          # uniform over top10?
          w=np.zeros_like(cC); K=10; w[np.argsort(cC)[-K:]]=1.0/K; w[np.argmax(cC)]? Ensure sum1
       g=grad_from_weights(h,w,dx)
       # optional reparameterize to keep sum: add Lagrange? We'll project.
       t+=1
       m_state = b1*m_state + (1-b1)*g
       v_state = b2*v_state + (1-b2)*g*g
       mhat=m_state/(1-b1**t); vhat=v_state/(1-b2**t)
       h = h - lr * mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
       h = project_feasible(h,n)
       # adaptive beta? if active range?
    return best_h,best_c

def project_feasible_fast(h,n):
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    diff=target-np.sum(h)
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       # try add to all (should not clip if target between current min/max? But may be at bounds)
       h = h + diff/n
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # second pass distribute to interior for exact
    diff=target-np.sum(h)
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       if diff>0:
          mask=h<1-1e-15
       else: mask=h>1e-15
       if np.any(mask):
           h[mask] += diff/np.sum(mask)
       else:
           h += diff/n
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final if still diff, adjust one entry
    diff=target-np.sum(h)
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       idx=np.argmin(np.where(diff>0, 1-h, h)) # argmin of slack? need top-level
       h[idx]+=diff
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
But final may make infeasible if set clip. Better: For exact sum, find an index with slack > abs(diff) (if diff positive slack=1-h, if negative slack=h), add diff there. def project_feasible(h,n): robust iterative capping:
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy(); h=np.clip(h,0,1); target=n/2.0
    # main loop up to 20
    for _ in range(20):
      s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
      if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
      if diff>0:
         mask=h<1-1e-14
      else:
         mask=h>1e-14
      if not np.any(mask):
         # distribute equally, maybe clip
         h += diff/n
         h=np.clip(h,0,1)
         continue
      # calculate max add per masked so not clip: if diff>0, cap=(1-h[mask]).max(); add=diff/count; if add>cap, add to a subset? Simple: add = diff/count; if add>cap add to max slack? But this loop can handle.
      add=diff/np.sum(mask)
      # To avoid overshoot when some near bound, cap add to min slack for diff>0 or h for diff<0 among mask? Actually if add > min slack, some will clip; we can limit add to min slack and iterate.
      if diff>0:
         add = min(add, (1.0 - h[mask]).min() * 0.999)
      else:
         add = max(add, h[mask].min() * -0.999) # negative
      h[mask]+=add
    # final adjust one feasible index
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       if diff>0:
          # choose index with max 1-h
          idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmax(h))
       h[idx]+=diff
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # due to float, final exact? We can do one more:
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       # adjust all by diff/n tiny, clip likely okay
       h += diff/n
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h

Implement top-level:
def force_sum(h,n):
   h=np.clip(h,0,1).copy()
   target=n/2.0
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)>1e-13:
      if diff>0:
         room=1.0-h
         order=np.argsort(-room)
      else:
         room=h
         order=np.argsort(-room)
      # distribute up to room
      rem=abs(diff)
      sign=1.0 if diff>0 else -1.0
      for i in order:
         if rem<=1e-14: break
         amt=min(room[i],rem)
         if amt>0: h[i]+=sign*amt; rem-=amt
         if rem<=1e-14: break
   return h
This loops up to n but only when projection off. Good. Project can be simpler: clip, add diff to all, clip, force_sum.
def project_feasible(h,n):

def bits_from_h(h,n):
   target=n//2
   h=np.asarray(h)
   # if n even. If target=0? not
   # choose top target indices:
   if target <=0: return 0
   if target >=n: return (1<<n)-1
   part=np.argpartition(h, target-1)[:target]
   bits=0
   # use loop maybe vectorized: for i in part: bits |= 1<<int(i)
   for i in part: bits |= 1 << int(i)
   return bits

def binary_evolution(n, seed, deadline, pop=48):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   # initialize population: random balanced, parent bits, etc.
   pop_bits = list unique.
   Evaluate all.
   best...
   generation=0
   while time<deadline:
     generation+=1
     # sort by fitness
     pop.sort(key=fitness)
     newpop=[]
     # keep elite 5
     for i in range(min(5,len(pop))): newpop.append(pop[i][0])
     # for filling, choose parents tournament, mutate 1-3 swaps, maybe crossover
     while len(newpop)<pop_size and time<deadline:
        p1=random best 30, p2=random best 50
        child = p1
        # crossover: if p2 different, take random half? but balanced repair
        # simple mutate: flip k pairs random
        new_bits=mutate(child, rng, n, k)
        add if valid
     # local improve each child a few steps? maybe
     replace pop

def binary_hill(bits,n,deadline,rng,max_steps):

      generate random swap
      newc,newm=eval
      if newc<curc: accept
      else maybe best if below; else no.
      if no improvement in last X, break or random restart.

def polish_with_slsqp(h0,n,beta,deadline,maxiter):
   global _G_N,_G_BETA,_G_TARGET,_G_DEADLINE
   _G_N=n; _G_BETA=beta; _G_TARGET=n/2.0; _G_DEADLINE=deadline
   h=project_feasible(h0,n)
   try:
     res=minimize(slsqp_obj,h,jac=slsqp_jac,bounds=[(0,1)]*n,constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':slsqp_con}],method='SLSQP',options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'iprint':0},callback=slsqp_callback)
     h=project_feasible(res.x,n)
   except Exception:
     h=project_feasible(h0,n)
   return h

Top-K weights:
def topk_weights(cC,K):
   w=np.zeros_like(cC)
   if K<=0: return w
   idx=np.argsort(-cC)[:K]
   w[idx]=1.0/K
   return w

def project_feasible(h,n):
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   diff=target-h.sum()
   # if after adding diff to all would stay within bounds, do it
   if diff>0 and h.max()+diff/n <=1+1e-15 and h.min()>=0... h += diff/n; h=np.clip
   else more robust.

Implement:
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(5):
       s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: return h
       # quick uniform adjust
       cand = h + diff/n
       if (cand.min() >= -1e-15) and (cand.max() <= 1+1e-15):
           h = np.clip(cand,0,1); return h
       # add to interior capped
       if diff>0:
          mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-12
       else:
          mask = h > 1e-12
       if not np.any(mask):
          # all at bounds, but target maybe half; adjust all? shouldn't
          h = np.clip(cand,0,1); continue
       # compute max allowed per masked
       if diff>0:
          room = 1.0 - h[mask]
          # We want add diff distributed proportional to room? To maintain near shape, distribute to all interior up to room.
          # If total room > diff, add = diff/room.sum() * room? This changes high slack more.
          frac = diff / room.sum()
          h[mask] += room * frac
       else:
          room = h[mask]
          frac = (-diff) / room.sum()
          h[mask] -= room * frac
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # final exact by distributing residual greedily
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       if diff>0: room=1.0-h; order=np.argsort(-room)
       else: room=h; order=np.argsort(-room)
       rem=abs(diff); sign=1.0 if diff>0 else -1.0
       for i in order:
          if rem<=1e-14: break
          amt=min(room[i], rem)
          if amt>0:
             h[i]+=sign*amt; rem-=amt
    return h
This uses argsort only if residual after proportional step. Good.
But inside loop with proportional to room may under-add if total room < diff? If total room < diff, then frac>1, h[mask]=1 (if positive) and residual remains; next loop mask empty except maybe? Then final greedy. Good.

For binary h exactly n/2 ones, projection force_sum no diff.

def resample_h(h, n):
   m=len(h); if m==n: return h.copy()
   xm=(np.arange(m)+0.5)*2.0/m
   xn=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
   return project_feasible(np.interp(xn,xm,h), n)

Let's write `smooth_gradient` carefully:
def smooth_gradient(h, n, beta):
    dx=2.0/n
    corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    cC=corr*dx
    m=float(cC.max())
    e=np.exp(beta*(cC-m))
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       w=np.zeros_like(cC); w[int(np.argmax(cC))]=1.0
    else:
       w=e/s
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    mid=n-1
    wm=w[mid]
    if wm != 0.0:
       grad += wm*(1.0-2.0*h)
    for d in range(1,n):
       wa=w[mid-d]
       wb=w[mid+d]
       s2=wa+wb
       if s2 <= 1e-12: continue
       mlen=n-d
       xseg=h[:mlen]
       yseg=h[d:]
       if wa != 0.0:
          grad[:mlen] += wa*(1.0 - yseg)
          grad[d:] += -wa*xseg
       if wb != 0.0:
          grad[:mlen] += -wb*yseg
          grad[d:] += wb*(1.0 - xseg)
    return dx*grad
Could combine wa/wb to reduce operations:
   grad[:mlen] += wa*(1-yseg) - wb*yseg
   grad[d:] += -wa*xseg + wb*(1-xseg)
This creates temps. Use combined s skip. Fine.

def bits_to_array(bits,n):
   h=np.zeros(n)
   b=bits
   i=0
   while b:
      if b & 1: h[i]=1.0
      b >>=1; i+=1
      if i>=n: break
   return h

def random_balanced_bits(n,rng):
   target=n//2
   idx=rng.choice(n,target,replace=False)
   bits=0
   for i in idx: bits |= 1<<int(i)
   return bits

Let's detail `adam_polish`:
def adam_polish(h0,n,deadline,rng,maxiters=2000):
    h=project_feasible(h0,n).copy()
    best_c=compute_c5(h); best_h=h.copy()
    # phases: list of (mode, param1, param2, lr)
    phases=[('soft',30.0,0,0.015),('top',0.0,12,0.025),('soft',100.0,0,0.005),('top',0.0,4,0.01)]
    for mode,paramA,paramB,lr in phases:
       if time.time() >= deadline - 5: break
       it_target = maxiters//len(phases)
       m_state=np.zeros(n); v_state=np.zeros(n); t=0
       no_imp=0
       for it in range(it_target):
          if time.time() >= deadline - 2: break
          dx=2.0/n
          corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
          cC=corr*dx
          C=float(cC.max())
          if C < best_c - 1e-12:
             best_c=C; best_h=h.copy(); no_imp=0
          else:
             no_imp += 1
          if no_imp > 300:
             # perturb
             h = h + rng.normal(0.0,0.03,h.size)
             h = project_feasible(h,n)
             no_imp = 0
             continue
          if it % 50 == 49:
             # adapt learning? no
          # weights
          if mode=='soft':
             beta=paramA
             mval=float(cC.max())
             e=np.exp(beta*(cC-mval)); s=e.sum()
             if s>0 and np.isfinite(s): w=e/s
             else: w=np.zeros_like(cC); w[int(np.argmax(cC))]=1.0
          else:
             K=paramB
             w=np.zeros_like(cC)
             if K>=len(cC): idx=np.arange(len(cC))
             else: idx=np.argpartition(cC, -K)[-K:]
             w[idx] = 1.0/K
          g = weighted_gradient(h, w, dx)
          t += 1
          b1=0.9; b2=0.999
          m_state = b1*m_state + (1-b1)*g
          v_state = b2*v_state + (1-b2)*(g*g)
          mhat = m_state/(1.0-b1**t)
          vhat = v_state/(1.0-b2**t)
          h = h - lr * mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
          h = project_feasible(h,n)
       # after phase, if best_h better than h? set h=best_h for next phase? Maybe if current worse due Adam divergence, restart from best.
       if compute_c5(h) > best_c + 1e-9:

def binary_sa(bits,n,deadline,rng):
    cur=bits
    cur_c, cur_m=eval_bits(cur,n)
    best=cur; best_c=cur_c; best_m=cur_m
    T=3.0
    min_T=0.1
    no_improve=0
    last_best_time=time.time()
    it=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       it+=1
       # generate swap
       while True:
          a=rng.randint(n)
          ba=(cur>>a)&1
          b=rng.randint(n)
          if ba == 0:
             if ((cur>>b)&1)==1: break
          else:
             if ((cur>>b)&1)==0: break
       new=cur ^ (1<<a) ^ (1<<b)
       new_c,new_m=eval_bits(new,n)
       if new_c < best_c - 1e-15 or (abs(new_c-best_c)<1e-15 and new_m<best_m):
          best=new; best_c=new_c; best_m=new_m
          last_best_time=time.time()
          no_improve=0
       accept=False
       if new_m <= cur_m:
          accept=True
       else:
          delta=new_m-cur_m
          if delta <= int(T)+1: # T count units
             # accept prob exp(-delta/max(T,0.1))
             if rng.random() < math.exp(-delta / max(T,0.1)):
                accept=True
       if accept:
          cur=new; cur_c=new_c; cur_m=new_m
       if best is cur? no
       T=max(min_T, T*0.99985)
       if not accept and new_c>=cur_c:
          no_improve+=1
       else: no_improve? Hmm.
       if no_improve > 1500 or time.time()-last_best_time > 60:
          # jump
          cur=best
          for _ in range(rng.randint(2,6)):
             generate swap and apply
          no_improve=0
          T=max(T,2.0)
    return best,best_c

_G_DE_N=0; _G_DE_DEADLINE=0.0
def de_eval(p):
    n=_G_DE_N
    if n<=0: return 1.0
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
    h=0.5*np.ones(n)
    # p length 2*modes: amp, phase pairs
    for i in range(0,len(p)-1,2):
       a=p[i]; ph=p[i+1]; r=i//2+1
       h += a*(np.cos(math.pi*r*x + ph))
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    return compute_c5(h)
def de_cb(x, convergence):

def run_fourier_de(seed,n,deadline):

    except Exception: return None

`compute_c5`:
```
def compute_c5(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    n=h.size
    if n==0: return float('inf')
    dx=2.0/n
    corr=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    val=float(dx*np.max(corr))
    return val
```
`project_feasible` as above.
`force_sum` separate? Could inline in project. But top-level no nesting.

`force_sum`:
```
def force_sum(h,n):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    target=0.5*n
    diff=target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-13:
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-h
          order=np.argsort(-room)
          rem=diff; sign=1.0
       else:
          room=h
          order=np.argsort(-room)
          rem=-diff; sign=-1.0
       for idx in order:
          if rem<=1e-14: break
          amt=room[idx]
          if amt>rem: amt=rem
          if amt>0: h[idx]+=sign*amt; rem-=amt
    return h
```
But if `room[idx]` is nan? h finite. Good.

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(4):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: return force_sum(h,n)
       cand=h+diff/n
       if cand.min()>=-1e-14 and cand.max()<=1.0+1e-14:
          return force_sum(np.clip(cand,0.0,1.0), n)
       if diff>0:
          mask=h<1.0-1e-12
       else: mask=h>1e-12
       if not np.any(mask):
          h=np.clip(cand,0.0,1.0)
          continue
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-h[mask]
          tot=room.sum()
          if tot<=0: h=np.clip(cand,0.0,1.0); continue
          scale=diff/tot
          if scale>1.0: scale=1.0
          h[mask]+=room*scale
       else:
          room=h[mask]; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<=0: h=np.clip(cand,0.0,1.0); continue
          scale=(-diff)/tot
          if scale>1.0: scale=1.0
          h[mask]-=room*scale
       h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    return force_sum(h,n)
```
The loop may return before exact? force_sum ensures.

`resample_h`:
```
def resample_h(h,n):
   m=np.asarray(h).size
   if m==n: return project_feasible(h,n)
   xm=(np.arange(m)+0.5)*(2.0/m)
   xn=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
   y=np.interp(xn,xm,np.asarray(h,dtype=float))
   return project_feasible(y,n)
```

def select_seeds(seeds, max_count=12, per_n=2):
    if not seeds: return []
    s=sorted(seeds, key=seed_key)
    res=[]; cnt={}
    for c,n,h in s:
       cn=cnt.get(n,0)
       if cn>=per_n: continue
       if c>res[-1][0]+1e-5 if res else False? We can allow.
       res.append((c,n,h.copy())); cnt[n]=cn+1
       if len(res)>=max_count: break
    return res
```

`smooth_objective`, `smooth_gradient`, `weighted_gradient`.

In `weighted_gradient`, the weights correspond to cC values; if weights sum not 1 (due topK exactly 1), okay. It multiplies dx.
```
def weighted_gradient(h, weights, dx):
    n=h.size
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    mid=n-1
    wm=weights[mid]
    if wm != 0.0:
       grad += wm*(1.0-2.0*h)
    for d in range(1,n):
       wa=weights[mid-d]; wb=weights[mid+d]
       sw=wa+wb
       if sw <= 1e-14: continue
       mlen=n-d
       x=h[:mlen]; y=h[d:]
       grad[:mlen] += wa*(1.0-y) - wb*y
       grad[d:] += -wa*x + wb*(1.0-x)
    return dx*grad
```
Potential memory: `wa*(1.0-y) - wb*y` temp; okay.

`smooth_gradient` calls weighted_gradient after computing weights. To avoid recomputing corr? Need both objective and gradient in SLSQP; SLSQP calls objective and gradient separately; can't share. Fine.

`smooth_objective`:
```
def smooth_objective(h,n,beta):
    dx=2.0/n
    corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    cC=corr*dx
    m=float(np.max(cC))
    if beta<=1e-12: return m
    e=np.exp(beta*(cC-m))
    s=np.sum(e)
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return m
    return m + math.log(s)/beta
```
If beta large and m maybe 0? no.

def run_slsqp(h0,n,beta,deadline):
    global _G_N,_G_BETA,_G_TARGET,_G_DEADLINE
    _G_N=n; _G_BETA=beta; _G_TARGET=0.5*n; _G_DEADLINE=deadline
    h=project_feasible(h0,n)
    maxiter=220 if n<=160 else 140
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)] for _ in range(n)  # No lambda? list comprehension okay, not closure? It's a comprehension, allowed? It doesn't create lambda/closure? It may be considered closure? Better create loop:
    bounds=[]; for _ in range(n): bounds.append((0.0,1.0))
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_slsqp_con}]
    try:
       res=minimize(_slsqp_obj,h,jac=_slsqp_jac,bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,method='SLSQP',options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-9,'iprint':0},callback=_slsqp_cb)
       h=project_feasible(res.x,n)
    except Exception:
       h=project_feasible(h0,n)
    return h
```

`bits_from_h` uses np.argpartition. If target n//2 exact. For n even. If h has NaN? no.
```
def bits_from_h(h,n):
    target=n//2
    if target<=0: return 0
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    if target>=n: return (1<<n)-1
    idx=np.argpartition(h, target-1)[:target]
    bits=0
    for i in idx:
       bits |= 1 << int(i)
    return bits
```
If parent n odd and target exact n//2 but sum target n/2; picking top half of fractional. okay.

`eval_bits` break:
```
def eval_bits(bits,n):
    full=(1<<n)-1
    comp=full ^ bits
    maxc=0
    for k in range(1,n):
       if n-k <= maxc: break
       ca=(bits & (comp >> k)).bit_count()
       cb=(comp & (bits >> k)).bit_count()
       m=ca if ca>cb else cb
       if m>maxc: maxc=m
    return 2.0*maxc/n, maxc
```
Could there be a lag beyond break that has high count? Max possible n-k <= maxc, so cannot exceed. Good. But what about maxc from one orientation, other maybe? max possible for combined max is n-k same. Good.
If maxc=0 (impossible unless? h all? With half ones, some lag >0), loops all.

`generate_binary_swap`:
```
def random_swap_bits(bits,n,rng):
   while True:
      a=rng.randint(n)
      ba=(bits>>a)&1
      b=rng.randint(n)
      if (ba==0 and ((bits>>b)&1)==1) or (ba==1 and ((bits>>b)&1)==0):
          return bits ^ (1<<a) ^ (1<<b)
```
Could be infinite if all bits same, but target half so not. For speed, use local.
`jump_bits` uses multiple swaps.

`binary_sa`:
```
def binary_sa(bits,n,deadline,rng):
   cur=bits
   cur_c,cur_m=eval_bits(cur,n)
   best=cur; best_c=cur_c; best_m=cur_m
   T=3.0; minT=0.25; no=0; last_best=time.time()
   while time.time() < deadline:
      if time.time() > deadline: break
      new=random_swap_bits(cur,n,rng)
      new_c,new_m=eval_bits(new,n)
      if new_c < best_c - 1e-15 or (new_c <= best_c + 1e-15 and new_m < best_m):
          best=new; best_c=new_c; best_m=new_m; last_best=time.time(); no=0
      if new_m <= cur_m:
          cur=new; cur_c=new_c; cur_m=new_m
      else:
          delta=new_m-cur_m
          if delta <= 5 and rng.random() < math.exp(-delta / max(T,0.1)):
              cur=new; cur_c=new_c; cur_m=new_m
      # if many no acceptance? no update regardless? use time since best
      if time.time() - last_best > 45:
          # jump from best
          cur=best
          for _ in range(3):
             cur=random_swap_bits(cur,n,rng)
          T=max(T,2.0)
      T=max(minT, T*0.9997)
   # local polish best
   return binary_local_polish(best,n,min(deadline+10, time.time()+15),rng)
```

We can skip local polish or run if time? At end no time. We can have binary_sa use a smaller deadline and leave time for local. Simpler: `binary_sa` doesn't call local; run separately in run if time.

`binary_local_polish`:
```
def binary_local_polish(bits,n,deadline,rng,maxiters=50000):
   cur=bits; cur_c,cur_m=eval_bits
   best=cur; best_c=...
   for i in range(maxiters):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      new=random_swap_bits(cur,n,rng)
      new_c,new_m=eval_bits
      if new_c < best_c -1e-15 or ...: best=...
      if new_m <= cur_m:
         cur=new; ...
   return best,best_c

Let's implement `shift_sweep`:
```
def shift_sweep(h,n,deadline):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float); c=compute_c5(h); best=h.copy()
    for s in range(1,n):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       hs=np.roll(h,s)
       cs=compute_c5(hs)
       if cs < c - 1e-12: c=cs; best=hs.copy()
    return best,c
```

Let's implement `make_sine_start` with rng and n.
```
def make_sine_start(n,rng,n_modes=6):
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
   h=0.5*np.ones(n)
   for r in range(1,n_modes+1):
      amp=rng.uniform(-0.3,0.3)
      if r==1: amp = abs(amp)+0.1 if amp>=0 else -abs(amp)-0.1? We need not. Use amp = rng.uniform(0.1,0.35) maybe sign.
      ph=rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi)
      h += amp * np.cos(math.pi*r*x + ph)
   h += rng.uniform(-0.05,0.05)
   return project_feasible(h,n)
```
If many modes, h_raw can go outside [0,1] heavily, project clips, losing pattern. Use amp Max total 0.4. n_modes=5, each amplitude ≤0.15? We'll set:
 for r in range(1,n_modes+1): amp = rng.uniform(0.05,0.25)/(math.sqrt(r)) maybe.

```
def run_fourier_de(seed,n,deadline):
    global ...
    n_modes=5
    bounds=[]
    for r in range(n_modes): bounds.append((-0.35,0.35)); bounds.append((0,2π))
    _G_DE_N=n; _G_DE_DEADLINE=deadline
    try:
       res=differential_engine(de_eval,bounds,seed=seed, maxpop=min(60, 5*n_modes), maxiter=120, polish=False, callback=de_cb, tol=1e-5, init='latinhypercube')
       h=de_to_h(res.x)
       c=compute_c5(h)
       return h,c
    except Exception:
       return None
```
`maxpop=25`; evals 3000. Good.

Rudin-Shapiro sequence ±1 length 2^m:

Detailed run code:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start=time.time()
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    active_deadline = start + budget_s - 20.0
    best_h=None; best_c=float('inf'); best_n=0
    seeds=[]
    parent=None
    try:
       parent=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).ravel()
       if parent.size>0:
          parent=project_feasible(parent,parent.size)
          pc=compute_c5(parent)
          if np.isfinite(pc) and pc<best_c:
             best_c=pc; best_n=parent.size; best_h=parent.copy()
          if budget_s > 60:
              deadline = min(active_deadline, start + 30.0)
              hs,cs=shift_sweep(parent,parent.size,deadline)
              if np.isfinite(cs) and cs<best_c:...
          seeds,c = add_candidate(seeds,parent,parent.size)
    except Exception:
       parent=None
    # Generate starts
    nset=[128,160,192,224,256]
    if parent is not None: nset.insert(0,parent.size)
    nuniq=sorted(set(nset))
    for N in nuniq:
       if N<16 or N>300: continue
       if time.time() > active_deadline - 120: break
       if parent is not None:
          hN=resample_h(parent,N)
          seeds,c=add_candidate(seeds,hN,N)
          if c is not None and c<best_c: ...
          shear? if N<=192: add shift_sweep? maybe no.
          ref=hN[::-1]
          seeds,c=add_candidate(seeds,ref,N)
          if c... 
          # one cyclic shift
          s=N//5 if N>=10 else 1
          sh=np.roll(hN,s)
          seeds,c=add_candidate(seeds,sh,N)
       if N==128 or N==192:
          for _ in range(2):
             h=random_feasible_start(N,rng)
             seeds,c=add_candidate(seeds,h,N)
             if c...
             h2=make_sine_start(N,rng,5)
             seeds,c=add_candidate(seeds,h2,N)
             if c...
    # Binary
    rem = budget_s - (time.time()-start)
    if rem > 120:
       bin_budget=min(160.0, max(40.0, rem*0.12))
       bin_ns=[128,192]
       for N in bin_ns:
          if time.time() > active_deadline - 60: break
          if parent is not None: hstart=resample_h(parent,N)
          else: hstart=make_sine_start(N,rng,5)
          bits=bits_from_h(hstart,N)
          deadline = min(active_deadline-30, time.time()+bin_budget/len(bin_ns))
          if deadline < time.time()+8: continue
          best_bits, bc = binary_sa(bits,N,deadline,rng)
          if time.time() < active_deadline-15:
             best_bits,bc=binary_local_polish(best_bits,N,min(active_deadline-10,time.time()+10),rng)
          hbits=bits_to_array(best_bits,N)
          if time.time() < active_deadline-10:
             hbits,bc=shift_sweep(hbits,N,min(active_deadline-5,time.time()+15))
          seeds,c=add_candidate(seeds,hbits,N)
          if c is not None and c<best_c: ...
          seeds,c=add_candidate(seeds,hbits[::-1],N)
          if c ...
    # Fourier DE if time
    rem = budget_s - (time.time()-start)
    if rem > 100:
       n_de=192
       deadline = min(active_deadline-10, time.time()+70.0)
       h_de,c_de=run_fourier_de(seed+101,n_de,deadline)
       if h_de is not None:
          if c_de < best_c: ...
          seeds,c=add_candidate(seeds,h_de,n_de)
          if c... maybe shift
    # Polish
    rem = budget_s - (time.time()-start)
    if rem > 40 and best_h is not None:
       # ensure best_h in seeds
       seeds,c=add_candidate(seeds,best_h,best_n)
       to_polish=select_seeds(seeds,10,2)
       for idx in range(len(to_polish)):
          c_seed,n,h=to_polish[idx]
          if time.time() > active_deadline - 10: break
          if c_seed > best_c + 0.01: continue
          if idx<2: slice=70.0
          elif idx<6: slice=40.0
          else: slice=20.0
          slice=min(slice, active_deadline - time.time())
          if slice < 15: break
          hp,cp=polish_candidate(h,n,time.time()+slice,rng)
          if np.isfinite(cp) and cp<best_c:
             best_c=cp; best_h=hp.copy(); best_n=n
    # final
    if best_h is None:
       N=128
       best_h=make_sine_start(N,rng,5)
       best_h=project_feasible(best_h,N)
       best_n=N; best_c=compute_c5(best_h)
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    final_c=compute_c5(best_h)
    try:
       ec=evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
       if ec is not None:
          ec=float(ec)
          if ec < final_c: final_c=ec
    except Exception:
       pass
    return (best_h, final_c, best_n)
```

    if len(seeds) <= limit: return seeds
    s = sorted(seeds, key=seed_key)
    return s[:limit]

def add_candidate(seeds, h, n, limit=60):
    try:
        hp = project_feasible(h, n)
        c = compute_c5(hp)
    except Exception:
        return (seeds, None)
    if (not np.isfinite(c)) or c > 2.0:
        return (seeds, None)
    seeds.append((c, n, hp.copy()))
    if len(seeds) > limit:
        seeds = trim_seeds(seeds, limit)
    return (seeds, c)
```

`polish_candidate`:
```
def polish_candidate(h0,n,deadline,rng):
    h=project_feasible(h0,n)
    best_c=compute_c5(h); best_h=h.copy()
    # SLSQP low beta
    if deadline - time.time() > 15:
        h2=run_slsqp(h,n,60.0,min(deadline-10.0, time.time()+40.0))
        c2=compute_c5(h2)
        if c2<best_c: best_c=c2; best_h=h2.copy()
        h=h2.copy()
    # Adam
    if deadline - time.time() > 25:
        total_iters=2500 if n<=160 else 1500
        ad_deadline=min(deadline-15.0, time.time()+max(10.0, (deadline-time.time())*0.65))
        h3,c3=adam_polish(h,n,ad_deadline,rng,total_iters)
        if c3<best_c: best_c=c3; best_h=h3.copy()
        if compute_c5(h) > best_c + 1e-9: h=best_h.copy()
    # SLSQP high beta
    if deadline - time.time() > 15:
        h4=run_slsqp(h,n,220.0,deadline)
        c4=compute_c5(h4)
        if c4<best_c: best_c=c4; best_h=h4.copy()
    return best_h, best_c
```
If `run_slsqp` times out via callback, res.x might be last iterate; it returns current h even if no improvement. Good.

Potential: `run_slsqp` may be called with deadline in past; it will still run? Check `if deadline - time.time() > 15`. Good.

Now, binary functions details:
`binary_sa` parameter deadline; if deadline <= time.time(), returns initial eval? In run check.
```
def binary_sa(bits,n,deadline,rng):
    cur=bits
    cur_c,cur_m=eval_bits(cur,n)
    best=cur; best_c=cur_c; best_m=cur_m
    T=3.0
    last_best=time.time()
    while time.time() < deadline:
        new=random_swap_bits(cur,n,rng)
        new_c,new_m=eval_bits(new,n)
        improved_best=False
        if new_c < best_c - 1e-15:
            improved_best=True
        elif abs(new_c-best_c)<1e-15 and new_m < best_m:
            improved_best=True
        if improved_best:
            best=new; best_c=new_c; best_m=new_m; last_best=time.time()
        if new_m <= cur_m:
            cur=new; cur_c=new_c; cur_m=new_m
        else:
            delta=new_m-cur_m
            if delta <= 5 and rng.random() < math.exp(-delta/max(T,0.05)):
                cur=new; cur_c=new_c; cur_m=new_m
        if time.time() - last_best > 50.0:
            cur=best
            for _ in range(2):
                cur=random_swap_bits(cur,n,rng)
            T=max(T,1.5)
        T=max(0.1, T*0.9995)
    return best,best_c
```
If candidate is bad, last_best may not update for >50s, jump from best with 2 swaps. Good.
Potential infinite loop in `random_swap_bits` if only ones or zeros? But balanced. If bits not balanced due bug, could infinite. `bits_from_h` target n/2. Good.

def binary_local_polish(bits,n,deadline,rng,maxiters=20000):
   cur=bits; cur_c,cur_m=eval_bits
   best=cur; ...
   vals=get_bit_values(n)
   for i in range(maxiters):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       # random swap
       while True:...
       new=cur ^ vals[a] ^ vals[b]
       new_c,new_m=eval_bits
       if new_c < best_c - 1e-15 or ... best update
       if new_m < cur_m:
           cur=new; ...
   return best,best_c
```
No accept worse. Good.

`random_swap_bits`:
```
def random_swap_bits(bits,n,rng):
    vals=get_bit_values(n)
    while True:
       a=int(rng.randint(n))
       va=vals[a]
       b=int(rng.randint(n))
       if (bits & va)!=0:
           if (bits & vals[b])==0: return bits ^ va ^ vals[b]
       else:
           if (bits & vals[b])!=0: return bits ^ va ^ vals[b]
```
Good.

Binary SA uses `random_swap_bits` with new vals each call; could pass vals? Fine.

def de_eval(p):
    n=_G_DE_N
    if n<=0 or _G_DE_X is None: return 1.0
    x=_G_DE_X
    h=0.5*np.ones(n)
    r=1
    for i in range(0,len(p),2):
        if i+1>=len(p): break
        h += p[i]*np.cos(math.pi*r*x + p[i+1])
        r+=1
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    return compute_c5(h)
```

def run_fourier_de(seed,n,deadline):
    global _G_DE_N,_G_DE_DEADLINE,_G_DE_X
    if deadline <= time.time(): return None,None
    _G_DE_N=n; _G_DE_DEADLINE=deadline; _G_DE_X=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    n_modes=5
    bounds=[]
    for i in range(n_modes): bounds.append((-0.35,0.35)); bounds.append((0.0,2.0*math.pi))
    try:
       res=differential_engine(de_eval,bounds,seed=seed,maxpop=30,maxiter=120,polish=False,callback=de_cb,tol=1e-6)
       h=de_to_h(res.x)
       c=compute_c5(h)
       return h,c
    except Exception:
       return None,None

```python
import numpy as np
import math
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_engine

_G_N = 0
_G_BETA = 1.0
_G_TARGET = 0.0
_G_DEADLINE = 0.0

_G_DE_N = 0
_G_DE_DEADLINE = 0.0
_G_DE_X = None

_bit_masks = {}
_bit_values = {}


def seed_key(s):
    return s[0]


def get_full_mask(n):
    try:
        return _bit_masks[n]
    except Exception:
        m = (1 << n) - 1
        _bit_masks[n] = m
        return m


def get_bit_values(n):
    try:
        return _bit_values[n]
    except Exception:
        vals = []
        for i in range(n):
            vals.append(1 << i)
        _bit_values[n] = vals
        return vals


def compute_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    dx = 2.0 / n
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(dx * np.max(corr))


def force_sum(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            order = np.argsort(-room)
            rem = diff
            sign = 1.0
        else:
            room = h
            order = np.argsort(-room)
            rem = -diff
            sign = -1.0
        for idx in order:
            if rem <= 1e-14:
                break
            amt = room[idx]
            if amt > rem:
                amt = rem
            if amt > 0.0:
                h[idx] += sign * amt
                rem -= amt
    return h


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n

    for _ in range(4):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            return force_sum(h, n)

        cand = h + diff / n
        if cand.min() >= -1e-14 and cand.max() <= 1.0 + 1e-14:
            return force_sum(np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1.0), n)

        if diff > 0:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-12
            if not np.any(mask):
                h = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1.0)
                continue
            room = 1.0 - h[mask]
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 0.0:
                h = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1.0)
                continue
            scale = diff / tot
            if scale > 1.0:
                scale = 1.0
            h[mask] += room * scale
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-12
            if not np.any(mask):
                h = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1.0)
                continue
            room = h[mask]
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 0.0:
                h = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1.0)
                continue
            scale = (-diff) / tot
            if scale > 1.0:
                scale = 1.0
            h[mask] -= room * scale

        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    return force_sum(h, n)


def resample_h(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    m = h.size
    if m == n:
        return project_feasible(h, n)
    xm = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) * (2.0 / m)
    xn = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    y = np.interp(xn, xm, h)
    return project_feasible(y, n)


def trim_seeds(seeds, limit):
    if len(seeds) <= limit:
        return seeds
    s = sorted(seeds, key=seed_key)
    return s[:limit]


def add_candidate(seeds, h, n, limit=60):
    try:
        hp = project_feasible(h, n)
        c = compute_c5(hp)
    except Exception:
        return (seeds, None)
    if (not np.isfinite(c)) or c > 2.0:
        return (seeds, None)
    seeds.append((c, n, hp.copy()))
    if len(seeds) > limit:
        seeds = trim_seeds(seeds, limit)
    return (seeds, float(c))


def select_seeds(seeds, max_count=12, per_n=3):
    if not seeds:
        return []
    s = sorted(seeds, key=seed_key)
    res = []
    cnt = {}
    for c, n, h in s:
        cn = cnt.get(n, 0)
        if cn >= per_n:
            continue
        res.append((c, n, h.copy()))
        cnt[n] = cn + 1
        if len(res) >= max_count:
            break
    return res


def shift_sweep(h, n, deadline):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h)
    for s in range(1, n):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        hs = np.roll(h, s)
        cs = compute_c5(hs)
        if cs < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = cs
            best_h = hs.copy()
    return best_h, best_c


def random_feasible_start(n, rng):
    return project_feasible(rng.rand(n), n)


def make_sine_start(n, rng, n_modes=6):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    h = 0.5 * np.ones(n)
    for r in range(1, n_modes + 1):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30) / math.sqrt(float(r))
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            amp = -amp
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        h += amp * np.cos(math.pi * r * x + ph)
    h += rng.uniform(-0.04, 0.04)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def weighted_gradient(h, weights, dx):
    n = h.size
    grad = np.zeros(n)
    mid = n - 1

    wm = weights[mid]
    if wm != 0.0:
        grad += wm * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)

    for d in range(1, n):
        wa = weights[mid - d]
        wb = weights[mid + d]
        sw = wa + wb
        if sw <= 1e-14:
            continue
        mlen = n - d
        x = h[:mlen]
        y = h[d:]
        grad[:mlen] += wa * (1.0 - y) - wb * y
        grad[d:] += -wa * x + wb * (1.0 - x)

    return dx * grad


def smooth_objective(h, n, beta):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    cC = corr * dx
    m = float(np.max(cC))
    if beta <= 1e-12:
        return m
    e = np.exp(beta * (cC - m))
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or (not np.isfinite(s)):
        return m
    return m + math.log(s) / beta


def smooth_gradient(h, n, beta):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    cC = corr * dx
    m = float(np.max(cC))
    if beta <= 1e-12:
        w = np.ones_like(cC) / cC.size
    else:
        e = np.exp(beta * (cC - m))
        s = float(np.sum(e))
        if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
            w = e / s
        else:
            w = np.zeros_like(cC)
            w[int(np.argmax(cC))] = 1.0
    return weighted_gradient(h, w, dx)


def _slsqp_obj(x):
    if _G_N <= 0:
        return 1e6
    return smooth_objective(x, _G_N, _G_BETA)


def _slsqp_jac(x):
    if _G_N <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0)
    return smooth_gradient(x, _G_N, _G_BETA)


def _slsqp_con(x):
    return np.sum(x) - _G_TARGET


def _slsqp_cb(x):
    return time.time() > _G_DEADLINE


def run_slsqp(h0, n, beta, deadline):
    global _G_N, _G_BETA, _G_TARGET, _G_DEADLINE
    _G_N = n
    _G_BETA = float(beta)
    if _G_BETA <= 1.0:
        _G_BETA = 1.0
    _G_TARGET = 0.5 * n
    _G_DEADLINE = float(deadline)

    h = project_feasible(h0, n)

    if n <= 160:
        maxiter = 260
    else:
        maxiter = 150

    bounds = []
    for i in range(n):
        bounds.append((0.0, 1.0))

    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": _slsqp_con}]

    try:
        res = minimize(
            _slsqp_obj,
            h,
            jac=_slsqp_jac,
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            method="SLSQP",
            options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-9, "iprint": 0},
            callback=_slsqp_cb,
        )
        h = project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        h = project_feasible(h0, n)

    return h


def adam_polish(h0, n, deadline, rng, total_iters=2000):
    h = project_feasible(h0, n).copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h)
    best_h = h.copy()

    phases = (
        ("soft", 30.0, 0, 0.015),
        ("top", 0.0, 12, 0.025),
        ("soft", 100.0, 0, 0.006),
        ("top", 0.0, 4, 0.010),
    )

    per = max(60, total_iters // len(phases))

    for mode, paramA, paramB, lr in phases:
        if time.time() >= deadline - 3.0:
            break

        m_state = np.zeros(n)
        v_state = np.zeros(n)
        t = 0
        b1 = 0.90
        b2 = 0.999
        eps = 1e-8
        no_imp = 0

        for _ in range(per):
            if time.time() >= deadline - 1.0:
                break

            dx = 2.0 / n
            corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
            cC = corr * dx
            C = float(np.max(cC))

            if C < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = C
                best_h = h.copy()
                no_imp = 0
            else:
                no_imp += 1

            if no_imp > 350:
                h = h + rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, n)
                h = project_feasible(h, n)
                no_imp = 0
                continue

            if mode == "soft":
                beta = paramA
                mv = float(np.max(cC))
                e = np.exp(beta * (cC - mv))
                s = float(np.sum(e))
                if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
                    w = e / s
                else:
                    w = np.zeros_like(cC)
                    w[int(np.argmax(cC))] = 1.0
            else:
                K = int(paramB)
                w = np.zeros_like(cC)
                if K <= 0:
                    del w
                    continue
                if K >= cC.size:
                    idx = np.arange(cC.size)
                else:
                    idx = np.argpartition(cC, -K)[-K:]
                w[idx] = 1.0 / K

            g = weighted_gradient(h, w, dx)
            t += 1

            m_state = b1 * m_state + (1.0 - b1) * g
            v_state = b2 * v_state + (1.0 - b2) * (g * g)
            mhat = m_state / (1.0 - b1 ** t)
            vhat = v_state / (1.0 - b2 ** t)

            h = h - lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)
            h = project_feasible(h, n)

        if compute_c5(h) > best_c + 1e-8:
            h = best_h.copy()

    return best_h, best_c


def bits_from_h(h, n):
    target = n // 2
    if target <= 0:
        return 0
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    if target >= n:
        return get_full_mask(n)

    idx = np.argpartition(h, target - 1)[:target]
    vals = get_bit_values(n)
    bits = 0
    for i in idx:
        bits |= vals[int(i)]
    return bits


def bits_to_array(bits, n):
    h = np.zeros(n)
    vals = get_bit_values(n)
    for i in range(n):
        if bits & vals[i]:
            h[i] = 1.0
    return h


def eval_bits(bits, n):
    full = get_full_mask(n)
    comp = full ^ bits
    maxc = 0
    for k in range(1, n):
        if n - k <= maxc:
            break
        ca = (bits & (comp >> k)).bit_count()
        cb = (comp & (bits >> k)).bit_count()
        m = ca if ca > cb else cb
        if m > maxc:
            maxc = m
    return (2.0 * maxc / n), maxc


def random_swap_bits(bits, n, rng):
    vals = get_bit_values(n)
    while True:
        a = int(rng.randint(n))
        va = vals[a]
        b = int(rng.randint(n))
        if (bits & va) != 0:
            if (bits & vals[b]) == 0:
                return bits ^ va ^ vals[b]
        else:
            if (bits & vals[b]) != 0:
                return bits ^ va ^ vals[b]


def binary_local_polish(bits, n, deadline, rng, maxiters=20000):
    cur = bits
    cur_c, cur_m = eval_bits(cur, n)
    best = cur
    best_c = cur_c
    best_m = cur_m

    for _ in range(maxiters):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        new = random_swap_bits(cur, n, rng)
        new_c, new_m = eval_bits(new, n)

        if new_c < best_c - 1e-15 or (abs(new_c - best_c) < 1e-15 and new_m < best_m):
            best = new
            best_c = new_c
            best_m = new_m

        if new_m < cur_m:
            cur = new
            cur_c, cur_m = new_c, new_m

    return best, best_c


def binary_sa(bits, n, deadline, rng):
    cur = bits
    cur_c, cur_m = eval_bits(cur, n)
    best = cur
    best_c = cur_c
    best_m = cur_m

    T = 3.0
    last_best = time.time()

    while time.time() < deadline:
        new = random_swap_bits(cur, n, rng)
        new_c, new_m = eval_bits(new, n)

        if new_c < best_c - 1e-15 or (abs(new_c - best_c) < 1e-15 and new_m < best_m):
            best = new
            best_c = new_c
            best_m = new_m
            last_best = time.time()

        if new_m <= cur_m:
            cur = new
            cur_c = new_c
            cur_m = new_m
        else:
            delta = new_m - cur_m
            if delta <= 5 and rng.random() < math.exp(-delta / max(T, 0.05)):
                cur = new
                cur_c = new_c
                cur_m = new_m

        if time.time() - last_best > 50.0:
            cur = best
            cur = random_swap_bits(cur, n, rng)
            cur = random_swap_bits(cur, n, rng)
            T = max(T, 1.5)

        T = max(0.1, T * 0.9995)

    return best, best_c


def de_eval(p):
    n = _G_DE_N
    if n <= 0 or _G_DE_X is None:
        return 1.0
    x = _G_DE_X
    h = 0.5 * np.ones(n)
    r = 1
    for i in range(0, len(p) - 1, 2):
        h += p[i] * np.cos(math.pi * r * x + p[i + 1])
        r += 1
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    return compute_c5(h)


def de_to_h(p):
    n = _G_DE_N
    if n <= 0 or _G_DE_X is None:
        return np.zeros(0)
    x = _G_DE_X
    h = 0.5 * np.ones(n)
    r = 1
    for i in range(0, len(p) - 1, 2):
        h += p[i] * np.cos(math.pi * r * x + p[i + 1])
        r += 1
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def de_cb(x, convergence):
    return time.time() > _G_DE_DEADLINE


def run_fourier_de(seed, n, deadline):
    global _G_DE_N, _G_DE_DEADLINE, _G_DE_X
    if (n <= 0) or (deadline <= time.time()):
        return None, None

    _G_DE_N = n
    _G_DE_DEADLINE = float(deadline)
    _G_DE_X = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)

    n_modes = 5
    bounds = []
    for i in range(n_modes):
        bounds.append((-0.35, 0.35))
        bounds.append((0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))

    try:
        res = differential_engine(
            de_eval,
            bounds,
            seed=int(seed),
            maxpop=30,
            maxiter=130,
            polish=False,
            callback=de_cb,
            tol=1e-6,
        )
        h = de_to_h(res.x)
        c = compute_c5(h)
        return h, c
    except Exception:
        return None, None


def polish_candidate(h0, n, deadline, rng):
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(h)
    best_h = h.copy()

    if deadline - time.time() > 15.0:
        h2 = run_slsqp(h, n, 60.0, min(deadline - 10.0, time.time() + 45.0))
        c2 = compute_c5(h2)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()
        h = h2.copy()

    if deadline - time.time() > 25.0:
        if n <= 160:
            total_iters = 3000
        else:
            total_iters = 1700
        ad_deadline = min(deadline - 15.0, time.time() + max(12.0, (deadline - time.time()) * 0.65))
        h3, c3 = adam_polish(h, n, ad_deadline, rng, total_iters=total_iters)
        if c3 < best_c:
            best_c = c3
            best_h = h3.copy()
        if compute_c5(h) > best_c + 1e-8:
            h = best_h.copy()

    if deadline - time.time() > 15.0:
        h4 = run_slsqp(h, n, 240.0, deadline)
        c4 = compute_c5(h4)
        if c4 < best_c:
            best_c = c4
            best_h = h4.copy()

    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.time()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    active_deadline = start + float(budget_s) - 22.0

    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")
    best_n = 0
    seeds = []

    parent = None
    try:
        parent = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
        if parent.size > 0:
            parent = project_feasible(parent, parent.size)
            pc = compute_c5(parent)
            if np.isfinite(pc) and pc < best_c:
                best_c = pc
                best_n = parent.size
                best_h = parent.copy()

            if budget_s > 60.0:
                dd = min(active_deadline, start + 35.0)
                hs, cs = shift_sweep(parent, parent.size, dd)
                if np.isfinite(cs) and cs < best_c:
                    best_c = cs
                    best_n = parent.size
                    best_h = hs.copy()

            seeds, c = add_candidate(seeds, parent, parent.size)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    nset = [128, 160, 192, 224, 256]
    if parent is not None:
        nset.insert(0, int(parent.size))
    nuniq = sorted(set(nset))

    for N in nuniq:
        if N < 16 or N > 320:
            continue
        if time.time() > active_deadline - 130.0:
            break

        if parent is not None:
            hN = resample_h(parent, N)
            seeds, c = add_candidate(seeds, hN, N)
            if c is not None and c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_n = N
                best_h = hN.copy()

            if N <= 224:
                ref = hN[::-1].copy()
                seeds, c = add_candidate(seeds, ref, N)
                if c is not None and c < best_c:
                    best_c = c
                    best_n = N
                    best_h = ref.copy()

                sh = np.roll(hN, max(1, N // 5))
                seeds, c = add_candidate(seeds, sh, N)
                if c is not None and c < best_c:
                    best_c = c
                    best_n = N
                    best_h = sh.copy()

        if N == 128 or N == 192:
            h1 = random_feasible_start(N, rng)
            seeds, c = add_candidate(seeds, h1, N)
            if c is not None and c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_n = N
                best_h = h1.copy()

            h2 = make_sine_start(N, rng, 5)
            seeds, c = add_candidate(seeds, h2, N)
            if c is not None and c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_n = N
                best_h = h2.copy()

    rem = float(budget_s) - (time.time() - start)
    if rem > 120.0:
        bin_budget = min(260.0, max(50.0, rem * 0.16))
        bin_ns = [128, 192]
        for N in bin_ns:
            if time.time() > active_deadline - 70.0:
                break

            if parent is not None:
                hstart = resample_h(parent, N)
            else:
                hstart = make_sine_start(N, rng, 5)

            bits = bits_from_h(hstart, N)
            ed = min(active_deadline - 40.0, time.time() + bin_budget / float(len(bin_ns)))
            if ed > time.time() + 8.0:
                bits, bc = binary_sa(bits, N, ed, rng)

                if time.time() < active_deadline - 20.0:
                    bits, bc = binary_local_polish(bits, N, min(active_deadline - 10.0, time.time() + 12.0), rng)

                hbits = bits_to_array(bits, N)
                if time.time() < active_deadline - 12.0:
                    hbits, bc = shift_sweep(hbits, N, min(active_deadline - 5.0, time.time() + 18.0))

                seeds, c = add_candidate(seeds, hbits, N)
                if c is not None and c < best_c:
                    best_c = c
                    best_n = N
                    best_h = hbits.copy()

                seeds, c = add_candidate(seeds, hbits[::-1].copy(), N)
                if c is not None and c < best_c:
                    best_c = c
                    best_n = N
                    best_h = hbits[::-1].copy()

    rem = float(budget_s) - (time.time() - start)
    if rem > 110.0 and time.time() < active_deadline - 40.0:
        n_de = 192
        dd = min(active_deadline - 20.0, time.time() + 90.0)
        h_de, c_de = run_fourier_de(int(seed) + 101, n_de, dd)
        if h_de is not None:
            if np.isfinite(c_de) and c_de < best_c:
                best_c = c_de
                best_n = n_de
                best_h = h_de.copy()
            seeds, c = add_candidate(seeds, h_de, n_de)
            if c is not None and c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_n = n_de
                best_h = h_de.copy()

    rem = float(budget_s) - (time.time() - start)
    if best_h is not None and rem > 45.0:
        seeds, c = add_candidate(seeds, best_h, best_n)
        to_pol = select_seeds(seeds, 12, 2)

        for idx in range(len(to_pol)):
            if time.time() > active_deadline - 12.0:
                break
            c_seed, n, h = to_pol[idx]
            if c_seed > best_c + 0.02:
                continue

            if idx < 2:
                slic = 90.0
            elif idx < 6:
                slic = 50.0
            else:
                slic = 22.0

            slic = min(slic, active_deadline - time.time())
            if slic < 15.0:
                break

            hp, cp = polish_candidate(h, n, time.time() + slic, rng)
            if np.isfinite(cp) and cp < best_c:
                best_c = cp
                best_n = n
                best_h = hp.copy()

    if best_h is not None and time.time() < active_deadline - 35.0:
        rem2 = active_deadline - time.time()
        slice2 = min(110.0, rem2 * 0.45)
        if slice2 > 25.0:
            hp, cp = polish_candidate(best_h, best_n, time.time() + slice2, rng)
            if np.isfinite(cp) and cp < best_c:
                best_c = cp
                best_n = best_n
                best_h = hp.copy()

    if best_h is None:
        N = 128
        best_h = make_sine_start(N, rng, 5)
        best_h = project_feasible(best_h, N)
        best_n = N
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    final_c = compute_c5(best_h)

    try:
        ec = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        if ec is not None:
            ec = float(ec)
            if np.isfinite(ec) and ec < final_c:
                final_c = ec
    except Exception:
        pass

    return (best_h, final_c, best_n)
```
